106.4.14 版 
- 1 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
全球生成式 AI 模型開發商 Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻 
企業需求帶動營收高成長，算力成本考驗高估值

報告日期：2026.11.9 研究員：吳俞潔

10 月 26 日 Anthropic(ANT US) 向美國 SEC 提交 IPO 申請，11 月 2 日更
新第三季財報，市場預期公司將於 11 月底前上市，估值達 1.5 至 2 兆。
Anthropic 旗下 Claude 主要應用於企業 AI、 軟體開發及Agent 工作流程 ，
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求快速成長，26Q3 營收達 169.6 億(季增
47%)，2026 年前三季累計營收 332 億。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年營收有
望達 580 億 ， 若以Palantir 做為高成長的企業 AI 軟體作為基準，Anthropic
也受惠企業 AI 支出增加， 營收增速高於Palantir，但未來大量算力成本以及
現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司， 對應27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，Anthropic 
IPO 估值達 1.6 至 1.8 兆，引發讓市場對企業 AI 商業化及模型公司估值的想
像空間。 
不過，兆豐認為 Anthropic 雖然已經證明企業願意為 AI 模型付費，但目前
1.6 至 1.8 兆估值已經提前反應未來數年營收維持高速成長，以及算力成本
持續改善的預期。公司 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但因算力及基礎設施費用季增
77%，導致營益率由上季 19%降至 15%，顯示收入增加並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長。另外，公司已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾，未來仍然需要擴
大 Token 需求才能支撐相關支出，考量企業 AI 需求持續成長，但模型價格
競爭及算力成本有壓縮利潤率風險， 因此兆豐目前對Anthropic 的 IPO 估值
持中性看待。

Claude Code 帶動企業 AI 需求，Anthropic 收入主要來自 Token 用量 
Anthropic 成立於 2021 年，由前 OpenAI 研究人員 Dario Amodei 創立，主
要開發 Claude 系列 AI 模型，透過 Claude 訂閱服務、企業 API 及 Claude 
Code 產品取得收入。相比傳統 SaaS 以固定訂閱費用為主，Anthropic 80%
營收來自 Token 使用量計價 ， 訂閱收入占20%， 企業可以透過AWS、Google 
Cloud 的雲端平台採購 Claude 服務，CSP 客戶占公司收入 48%，這種模式
使得 Anthropic 能隨著企業 AI 使用量增加而擴大收入，但也導致營收受到
Token 價格及企業使用量變動影響。 
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求成長，過去 12 個月支出超過 10 萬的客
戶達 11,900 家，其中超過 1,900 家年支出超過 100 萬，250 家超過 1,000

### Page 2

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 2 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
萬，企業 AI 支出正轉向更大規模的付費使用，也帶動 Anthropic 營收快速成
長。

估值達 1.8 兆，Token 價格決定高成長能否持續 
Anthropic 2026 年前三季營收達 332 億(年增 12 倍)，26Q3 營收 170 億(季
增 47%)，但相比 Q2 季增 144%成長大幅放緩。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年
營收達 580 億，若以 Palantir 作為高成長企業 AI 軟體的估值參考 ，合理倍數
落在 27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，對應 IPO 估值 1.6 至 1.8 兆，兆豐認為雖然
Anthropic 營收成長的確遠高於多數軟體公司，Claude Code 及企業 API 的
高需求也支持未來營收持續擴張，但但模型訓練及推論需要持續投入大量算
力 ， 獲利率及現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司，以 2026 年 580 億營收估算，
隱含 28 至 31 倍 Forward P/S，反應了市場不只預期公司維持高成長，也預
期未來利潤率能隨著規模擴大而改善。 
Anthropic 80%收入來自 Token 用量計價 ， 未來營收不只取決於企業使用量 ，
也包含模型能否維持目前的高階定價， 外資8 月調查 100 名歐美大型企業 AI
預算決策者，92%預期未來 12 個月 Token 預算增加，但若 Token 價格上漲
50%，84%企業會優化使用或轉換其他模型，若價格下降 50%，86%企業則
願意增加使用量，反應企業 AI 需求仍有成長空間，但客戶對價格相當敏感，
尤其開放權重模型及低成本模型持續改善，企業有機會將部分工作轉向更為
便宜的模型，即使整體 Token 使用量增加，但開放權重和低成本模型競爭，
Anthropic 面臨單價競爭導致營收增速放緩的風險，因此兆豐認為目前 1.6 至
1.8 兆估值已經反應 Anthropic 長期維持高成長及較高 Token 價格的預期，
但如果模型競爭加劇，導致價格下降速度超過增加的使用量，將有估值下修
風險。

接近 9,000 億的算力承諾，收入成長未必同步轉為獲利 
Anthropic 為了支撐 Claude 模型訓練及企業推論需求 ， 持續擴大與Amazon、
Google、Microsoft 及其他 AI 基礎設施供應商的合作 ， 並且透過Nvidia GPU、
Google TPU 及 AWS Trainium 分散算力來源，Anthropic 目前即時可用算力
達 2.7GW，與 OpenAI 的 3.2GW 差距縮小，近期再透過 Nscale、Lambda
及 SpaceX 取得 1.1GW 的新增算力協議。不過，算力擴張也帶來龐大的長
期採購支出 ，Anthropic 已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾 ， 其中4,475 億預
計在 2030 年底前兌現， 未來12 個月到期的承諾達 698 億，合約有最低採購
要求，即使未來需求不如預期，公司仍然需要負擔相關成本。 
從財務表現來看，Anthropic 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但算力及基礎設施費用季
增 77%，營益率也由 19%下降至 15%，顯示新增收入並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長，雖然公司提到部分成本增加來自短期高價算力採購，未來隨著長
期合約及專用資料中心陸續上線，單位算力成本有機會下降，但兆豐認為即
使採購成本改善，也不代表利潤率一定回升，主要因 Anthropic 提前承諾大
規模算力支出，但未來 Token 收入仍然取決於企業實際使用量及模型定價，

### Page 3

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 3 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
若新增算力無法帶來足夠的 Token 收入 ， 固定採購成本將壓縮獲利及現金流。 
因此兆豐認為 Anthropic 目前估值不只反應營收持續高成長，也隱含未來算
力成本下降能帶動利潤率改善，但考量 26Q3 營收增速大幅放緩，營益率下
滑、未來長期算力採購承諾龐大，以及 Token 需求成長無法抵銷模型降價及
新增算力成本 ， 將壓縮利潤率 ， 不足以支撐更高的高成長成熟企業 AI 軟體公
司估值，因此兆豐對 1.6 至 1.8 兆的 IPO 估值持中性看待

（本資料僅供內部參考，不作為投資決策依據）

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。

對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

2026年 10月 30日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月雇用統計 
失業率は2.4％と前月から0.1％pt低下し、就業拡大が進展

経済調査部 エコノミスト 菊池 慈陽

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲
0.1％pt）し、7月の水準に戻った。失業者数は「自発的な離職」を中心に 2カ月ぶり
に減少（同▲10万人）し、就業者数は2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参
加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月から改善したとみられる。 
◼ 2026年9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と3カ月ぶり
に上昇した。新規求職申込件数が大きく減少したことで、新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同
＋0.13pt）と上昇した。 
◼ 先行きの雇用環境は堅調に推移しよう。労働供給が中長期的に減少していく可能性が
高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組みを
続けている。ただし、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢をめぐる不確実
性は依然として高く、長期化する原油高が企業収益を悪化させ、幅広い産業で労働需
要を下押しする恐れがある。

9月の完全失業率：2.4％と前月から低下、「自発的な離職」による失業者が大きく減少 
2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲0.1％pt）
し、7月の水準に戻った（図表2左上） 。失業者数は2カ月ぶりに減少（同▲10万人）し、就業
者数は 2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月
から改善したとみられる。 
失業者数を求職理由別に見ると、「自発的な離職」 （前月差▲13万人）が大きく減少し、全体
を押し下げた。「勤め先や事業の都合による離職」（同▲1万人）は小幅に減少した。他方、「定
年又は雇用契約の満了」（同＋1万人）と「新たに求職」（同＋1万人）は小幅に増加した（巻末
の雇用概況①下段左）。 
就業者数の前月からの変化を業種別に見ると、「製造業」と「医療，福祉」が大きく増加し、
全体を押し上げた。「卸売業，小売業」と「情報通信業」も増加した。他方、「建設業」やその
他の非製造業は減少した。（図表2左下）。 
雇用者数（役員を除く）を雇用形態別に見ると、正規雇用者は前月から 22万人増加した。
2026年春頃から増加傾向にあり、ピークだった2025年 11月の水準を上回った。非正規雇用者
は 5カ月ぶりに増加（前月差＋21万人）した（図表 2右下）。

9月の新規求人倍率：求職が大きく減少した一方で求人は増加し、前月から上昇 
2026年 9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は 1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上
昇した。新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同＋0.13pt）と上昇した（図表3左）。 
求人側の動きを見ると、有効求人数は前月から横ばいだった一方、新規求人数（前月比＋
1.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 4左） 。求職側では、有効求職者数（同▲1.4％）、新規
求職申込件数（同▲4.4％）はいずれも 3カ月ぶりに減少した。求職側は 2025年春頃から持ち
直しの動きが見られており、求人側も2026年春頃から増加傾向にある。 
雇用形態別に見ると、正社員の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.01倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と
3カ月ぶりに上昇した。新規求人倍率も 1.79倍（同＋0.11pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上昇した（図表
3右）。

4 / 8

先行き：堅調な推移を見込むも、中東情勢など雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない 
先行きの雇用環境は、堅調に推移することを見込んでいる。労働供給が中長期的に減少して
いく可能性が高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど1、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組
みを続けている。 
日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）の2026年 9月調査では、雇用人員判断
DI（全規模全産業、 「過剰」－「不足」 ）は「最近」が▲38％ptと 6月調査から 1％pt低下し
（人手不足感が強まり）、「先行き」は▲41％ptと更なる低下が予想されている（巻末の雇用概
況①上段右）。特に、中小企業（全産業）は「最近」が▲42％ptと6月調査から3％pt低下し、
「先行き」は▲45％ptだった。中小企業を中心に、人手不足感が強まっているとみられる。 
株式会社ナウキャストによる「HRog賃金 Now」の求人数指数を見ると、正社員とパート・ア
ルバイトのいずれも 2026年春頃から上昇傾向にある（巻末の雇用概況③下段左）。人手不足感
の強まりを受けて、今後も労働需要は堅調に推移するとみられる。 
他方、トランプ米政権による高関税政策（トランプ関税）1F
2や、長期化する日中関係の冷え込
みは、引き続き労働需要の下押し要因となるだろう。 
また、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢は依然として不確実性が高い状況が続
いており、とりわけ注意が必要だ。原油等の供給網の正常化は当面見込みにくく、輸入原油価
格が高止まりしている。原油高は原材料費や物流費などの上昇を通じて企業収益を悪化させ3、
幅広い産業で労働需要を下押しする可能性がある。

1 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）が公表した第 7回（最終）回答集計結果では、定期昇給相当込みの賃上げ率
（加重平均）が企業規模計で 5.01％と、前年（5.25％）をやや下回るものの、高水準を維持している。300人
未満の中小組合では 4.69％と前年（4.65％）をわずかながら上回り、中小企業の積極的な姿勢が目立った。詳
細は、日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3年連続の 5％超え！～2026春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結
果について～」（2026年7月3日）を参照。 
2 トランプ米政権は通商法 122条に基づく措置が失効する 7月 24日に新たな関税措置を発動した（日本経済新
聞 電子版「トランプ政権が新関税発動、日本 12.5％ 中間選挙へ看板政策継続」（2026年7月 24日））。 
3 原油高による物価の上昇については、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年
8月」（大和総研レポート、2026年8月 24日）を参照。

Document 103: 2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2027 年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 
～労働側、経営側とも昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 連合は2027 年春闘基本構想で、 定昇込みの賃上げ要求を 「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベア等の
賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とする目安を示した。前年よりも要求姿勢をやや強めて
おり、５％台の賃上げの定着に加え、さらなる引き上げを目指す姿勢がうかがえる。 
○ 経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経労委報告の骨子案では賃上げ
モメンタムの「浸透・定着」が掲げられ、高い賃上げの継続に加え、中小企業への波及を重
視する姿勢が示されている。 
○ 日本経済研究センターのＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査によると、民間エコノミストは 2027 年
春闘の賃上げ率を4.96％と予想（厚生労働省ベース）。前年から小幅に鈍化するものの、高
い伸びが続く見通しであり、４年連続で５％台の賃上げを実現できるかが焦点となる。 
○ 春先にはイラン情勢悪化による賃上げ鈍化が懸念されていたが、現時点で労使双方の姿勢に
トーンダウンはみられない。企業業績の底堅さや構造的な人手不足が高い賃上げを支えてお
り、2027 年春闘の初動のモメンタムは良好と評価できる。

連合は、「６％中心、５％以上」と昨年からトーンをやや強める 
2027 年春闘の集中回答日は2027 年の３月中旬だが、実際の交渉に向けての動きは既に始まりつ
つある。 
本日、連合は「2027 春季生活闘争基本構想」を公表した。春闘に向けて最初に注目される重要
イベントであり、翌年の春闘に向けた労働組合側の基本理念や方針、重点課題を示すものであ
る。この基本構想をみることで、労働側が翌年の春闘にどの程度強気な姿勢で臨もうとしている
かを早い段階で確認することができ、春闘全体のトーンをつかむうえで重要な材料となる。 
今回の基本構想の最大の注目点は、定期昇給相当分を含めて「６％中心、５％以上」 、ベースア
ップ等の賃上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、より高い賃上げを目指す姿勢を示したことで
ある。「５％以上」という要求水準自体は４年連続（2023 年は「５％程度」）となるが、今回は
「６％中心」との表現が新たに加わった。ベア等の賃上げ分についても、従来の「３％以上」に

### Page 2

2/5 
「４％中心」が加えられている。前年の2026 年春闘では、全体で「５％以上」 、中小で「６％以
上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者では「７％」を目安とする、いわゆる「５・６・７」の要求が
示されていた。今回、全体についても６％を明確に意識する形となったことは、実質的には５％
を最低ラインとしつつ、より高い賃上げを促す姿勢を強めたものと受け取れる。単に高い賃上げ
を続けるだけでなく、その水準をさらに引き上げようとする意欲がうかがえる。

こうした姿勢の背景にあるのが、ここ数年の高い賃上げ実績である。2026 年春闘では連合ベー
スの賃上げ率の実績が5.01％となり、３年連続で５％台の高い賃上げが実現した。５％という水
準がかつてほど実現困難な目標ではなくなりつつあるなか、連合としても前年と同じ要求目安を
繰り返すだけでは、さらなる賃上げを促すメッセージとして十分ではないと判断した可能性があ
る。５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持するだけでなく、６％近辺を意識して賃上げ率のさらなる引
き上げを目指す段階に入ったというメッセージと位置付けられる。 
賃上げを取り巻く環境も、こうした強気の姿勢を支えている。構造的な人手不足が続くなか、
企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き重要な経営課題であり、賃金引き上げの必要性は高
い。また、物価上昇率は2026 年度後半にかけて再び高まるとみられ、家計の生活コスト負担は重
い。名目賃金が増加しても、物価上昇に追いつかなければ生活水準の改善にはつながらない。実
質賃金を安定的にプラスにするためにも、労働側が高い賃上げを求める理由は十分にある。 
加えて、賃上げの広がりという点でも課題は残る。2026 年春闘では中小組合の賃上げ率が
4.69％と高い伸びを確保したものの、全体の5.01％には届かなかった。大企業と中小企業の間に
はなお差があり、正規雇用と非正規雇用の賃金格差も残っている。連合にとっては、高い賃上げ
春闘年 連合の要求目安（定昇相当分込み） 連合ベース 厚労省ベース
2020 ４％程度 1.90％ 2.00％
2021 ４％程度 1.78％ 1.86％
2022 ４％程度 2.07％ 2.20％
2023 ５％程度 3.58％ 3.60％
2024 ５％以上 5.10％ 5.33％
2025 ５％以上 5.25％ 5.52％
2026 ５％以上 5.01％ 5.18％
2027 ６％中心、５％以上 — —
(出所）連合「春季生活闘争基本構想」、連合「春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結果」、厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」
連合の賃上げ要求目安と春闘賃上げ率
実績

### Page 3

3/5 
を実現するだけでなく、それを幅広い企業や労働者に波及させることも引き続き重要な課題であ
る。今回の基本構想でも、賃金実態を把握できないなどの事情がある中小組合については
「20,000 円以上・６％以上」 、有期・短時間・契約等労働者については「７％」を目安とする方針
が示された。こうした点を踏まえれば、今回の基本構想は、高い賃上げの持続と一段の引き上
げ、そして賃上げの裾野の拡大を目指すものと評価できる。 
また、今回の基本構想では、新たに2030 年の賃金水準を2026 年比で15％程度引き上げる中期
目標も設定された。毎年の賃上げ率だけでなく、中期的に目指す賃金水準を明示することで、高
い賃上げを一過性のものに終わらせず、継続させようとする姿勢がうかがえる。 
なお、近年は個別組合の実際の賃上げ要求率がすでに６％近くに達しており、 「６％中心」とい
う今回の表現には、こうした実態を追認する意味合いもある。今回の基本構想からは、連合の強
い賃上げ意欲が確認できる一方、実際の賃上げ率が前年を上回るかどうかはまた別問題である。
今後は、主要産業別労働組合組織や個別組合の要求水準が前年からどの程度引き上げられるか
（られないか）が注目される。

経営側も賃上げの継続に前向き 
経営側も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。経団連が毎年１月に公表する「経
営労働政策特別委員会報告（経労委報告） 」は、春闘に向けた経営側の基本スタンスを示すもので
あり、賃上げ交渉の方向性を占う上で重要な資料となる。2027 年春闘に向けた経労委報告につい
ては、現時点で原案は明らかになっていないものの、９月上旬には骨子案の内容が報道されてい
る。それによると、賃上げモメンタムの「浸透・定着」を掲げ、高い賃上げの継続と中小企業へ
の波及を促す方針とされる。 
経団連は2023 年を賃上げの「起点」 、2024 年を「加速」 、2025 年を「定着」 、2026 年を「さらな
る定着」の年と位置付けてきた。今回の「浸透・定着」という表現からは、高い賃上げを継続す
るとともに、その動きを中小企業などに広げることを重視する姿勢がうかがえる。また、経団連
の筒井会長は９月７日の記者会見で、 「ベースアップ実施の検討を引き続きスタンダードと位置付
けていきたい」と述べ、賃上げのモメンタムを維持する考えを示している。 
数か月前には、イラン情勢の悪化による原材料費や物流費の上昇が企業収益を圧迫し、2027 年
春闘では経営側が賃上げに慎重な姿勢を強めることも懸念されていた。しかし、現時点で経営側
の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられない。 
なお、経労委報告では、原案から正式な報告にかけての修正は例年あまり見られないが、骨子
案から原案にかけては内容が多少修正されることもある。今後、10 月末～11 月中頃までに報道さ
れるとみられる経労委報告の原案が明らかになれば、経営側のスタンスをより具体的に判断でき
るだろう。

エコノミスト予想は4.96％、５％を巡る攻防か 
10 月14 日に日本経済研究センターが公表した「ＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査」では、民間エコノ

### Page 4

4/5 
ミスト 34 人を対象に2027 年春闘の賃上げ率について特別調査を実施している。これによると、
予測値の平均は4.96％となり、2026 年実績の5.18％を 0.22％ポイント下回る見通しとなった。
もっとも、前年から小幅に鈍化するとはいえ、ほぼ５％という歴史的に高い水準であり、2027 年
も高い賃上げが続くとの見方が中心である。なお、ここでの賃上げ率は厚生労働省「民間主要企
業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」ベースであり、連合の集計とは対象が異なる。 
予測の内訳をみると、定期昇給分が1.69％、ベースアップ分が3.27％となっており、ベアにつ
いても３％を超える伸びが見込まれている。また、予測値の高位８機関平均は5.23％、低位８機
関平均は4.68％となっている。予測には一定の幅があるものの、高い賃上げが続くという点では
概ね認識は共有されている。

2027 年春闘で賃上げ率が前年からやや鈍化すると予想される背景には、2026 年の物価上昇率が
2025 年より低く、物価高に対応するための賃上げ圧力が幾分弱まることがあると考えられる。ま
た、数年にわたる高い賃上げによって人件費負担が累積していることに加え、イラン情勢悪化を
背景とした原材料費や物流費の上昇も、企業の賃上げ余力を抑制する要因となり得る。一方、企
業業績が足元まで好調を維持していることや、構造的な人手不足と採用競争の激化は引き続き賃
上げを促す要因であり、賃上げ率が大きく鈍化する可能性も低いとみられる。 
ちなみに、昨年の同時期のＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査でも、2026 年春闘の賃上げ率は4.81％
（2025 年10 月調査）と、前年実績からの鈍化が予想されていた。しかし、実際には5.18％と予
想を上回り、３年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現した。今回も前年からの小幅な鈍化が見込まれて
いるが、予測平均は昨年の同時期をやや上回っている。イラン情勢の悪化という新たな懸念材料
があるなかでも、エコノミストの間では高い賃上げが続くとの見方が維持されていることが分か
（出所）日本経済研究センター「ESPフォーキャスト調査（2026年10月調査）」
（注）2027年は予測値、2026年までの実績は厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結状況」

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
る。 
こうしたなか、予測平均の4.96％について、前年からの「鈍化」を強調するか、5％近傍という
「高水準の持続」を重視するかは、評価が分かれるところだろう。いずれにせよ、2027 年春闘で
は、３年連続で実現した５％台の賃上げを４年目も維持できるか、５％を巡る攻防が一つの焦点
となる。今後、経労委報告原案の判明により経営側の方針が明らかになるほか、主要産業別労働
組合組織の要求方針などが明らかになるにつれて、賃上げ率の見通しもより具体化していくこと
になるだろう。 
なお、この調査とは別に、毎年11 月～12 月にかけて、民間シンクタンク・調査機関から翌年の
春闘賃上げ率の予測が出されることが多い （筆者は 11 月に発表予定） 。 予測をレポートとして作成
することもあれば、 経済見通しレポートのなかで言及されることもあり、春闘賃上げ率の見通しに
ついて、この時期に市場の相場観が徐々に形成されていく。

2027 年春闘の初動は良好 
以上を踏まえると、2027 年春闘の「初動のモメンタム」は、現時点では良好と評価できる。イ
ラン情勢が悪化した数か月前には、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益の圧迫を通じて、
2027 年春闘の賃上げ率が大きく鈍化するとの懸念もあった。しかし、ここまでの動きをみる限
り、労働側、経営側のいずれについても、前年と比べて賃上げ姿勢が慎重化している様子はみら
れない。連合が定昇込み、ベアともに前年より強い要求目安を打ち出し、さらに高い賃上げを志
向する一方、経団連も高い賃上げの継続と中小企業への波及に前向きな姿勢を示している。 
その背景には、原油などの代替調達が進み、資源の調達難が生産活動を大きく制約する事態が
回避されたことや、ＡＩ需要の拡大にも支えられ、企業業績が総じて堅調さを維持していること
があるとみられる。また、構造的な人手不足や採用競争の激化によって、企業が人材確保・定着
のために賃金を引き上げる必要性は依然として高い。物価上昇が続くなか、実質賃金の改善を求
める声が強いことも、高い賃上げに向けての動きを後押ししているとみられる。 
もっとも、原材料費や物流費の上昇による企業収益への悪影響が今後さらに広がる可能性はあ
り、先行きを楽観視することはできない。また、現時点で確認できるのは労使双方の基本的な姿
勢であり、具体的な要求や回答の水準はまだ明らかになっていない。今後は、経団連「経労委報
告」の原案や主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針、早期に賃上げを表明する企業の動向などを通
じて、今回確認された前向きな姿勢が実際の賃上げにつながるかを見極める必要がある。

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E

the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Document 147: 261102_ms_GUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_ms_GUC.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
Axion CPU demand remains strong and on track: We noted  in a prior report  that
demand for Google's Axion CPUs, which GUC helps design, remains robust.
However, we now see further upside in both 2027 and 2028 from our previous
forecast. Also, some investors have questioned whether the Axion3 schedule has
been pushed out. Our  recent supply chain checks suggest that Axion3 has already
reached wafer-in and is on track for wafer-out in mid-2027. Axion CPU could reach 4-

5mn units of  Axion2 equivalent CPU in 2027, given that Axion3 includes two
computing dies but also adopts TSMC 3nm process. We now forecast volume at
4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3 in 2027, then 3mn units of Axion3 in
2028. We project ASP at US$700 and US$1400, respectively.
We also see upside surprise from Cobalt CPU: We believe the CPU demand upside
is coming not just from Axion but also Microsoft's Cobalt CPU. GUC continues to
get more front-end wafer support from TSMC; given strong CPU demand, we now

forecast >1mn units of Cobalt 200 in 2027 with ASP of ~US$700. Our industry
checks also suggest that Cobalt 300 is on track for mass production in 2028, which
might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die
CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be ~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
3Q26 revenue beat from CPU customers' demand pull-forward and new
customer: The company posted its  3Q26 results: revenue beat our estimate

strongly (by 23%);  the upside comes mostly from the largest CPU customer's pulling
demand forward  (50-55% of 3Q26 total revenue) and also the new US AI customer,
which has adopted the 5nm process and SoW (system-on-wafer) at TSMC. In 4Q26,
we expect a major Chinese auto company to start to contribute revenue. With the
ongoing robust CPU demand, we now expect 4Q26 revenue to be up 20% QoQ. See
Exhibit 3  for our 3Q26 earnings review.
Reiterate OW and raise price target to NT$8600: This 37% increase mostly

reflects the upside we have seen from CPU demand. However, aside from CPU
demand, we also see strong revenue from Tesla, Meta, Horizon, etc.   in 2028. Thus,
we expect 2025-28 revenue CAGR to reach 120%. Our new price target implies P/E
of 52x for 2027e and 27x for 2028e, which we think is justified by its strong revenue
growth.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
Axion CPU should be the largest customer for GUC in 2026-2028: We therefore now
forecast only 1.5mn units of Google Axion2 CPU in 2026, given the demand pull-in. By
assuming chip ASP at ~US$700, we now see 2026 Google CPU contributing slightly more
than US$1000mn.
For 2027, we have been seeing strong rises in demand expectations for Axion CPU,
including  the ramp of the next-generation Axion3. We now expect Axion CPU revenue of

close to US$3.5bn, including 4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3. In addition,
we also expect the chip ASP of Axion3 to double from Axion2 given the increase of 3nm
computing dies from one to two.
For 2028, we now forecast  3mn units of Axion3, slightly lower than Axion2's annual run
rate of 4mn units, mostly also driven by the doubling of computing dies.
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin for Axion CPU – likely around 10% –
because this is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design

support (e.g., silicon interposer and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for
the ASIC designs. We also expect margin for Axion3 to be slightly lower than for Axion2,
given higher chip ASP or larger project size.
Cobalt demand could  also grow 3-4x in 2027: GUC also helps with Microsoft's Cobalt
200/300 CPU. We now forecast at least US$1bn of GUC revenue contribution from
Cobalt 200 and Maia 200 in 2027. Also, our industry checks also suggest that Cobalt 300

is on track for mass production in 2028, which might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By
assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be
~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
Meta ASIC (MTIA 600): In our AI supply chain tracker, we noted our belief that GUC
could win one of the Meta ASIC projects, led by the Rivos team, and we now think it might
be MTIA 600, based on supply chain checks. The AI ASIC targets tape-out in 1H27, with

potential CoWoS out in 2028.
Note: these forecasts are from Asia Pacific research and incorporate supply chain and
industry checks.

Estimate revision summary
We raise   our EPS estimates 17% for 2026, 18% for 2027, and 50% for 2028: Our
increases come mostly from the strength in CPU as well as other AI projects' contribution,
including Meta ASIC, US design house adopting SoW, etc. With turnkey revenue share
seen increasing in both 2027 and 2028, we now assume that gross margin will fall from
21.4% in 2026 to 15.4% in 2028. However, we expect firmer operating margin this year,

thanks to a policy change regarding employee bonus; although this assumption dips in
2027, it rebounds in 2028 because of larger revenue scale with better operating leverage.
The same trend is visible in  net margin.

Price target rises 37%, to NT$8,600
This reflects our 2027-28 estimate revisions. Although we are cutting our terminal growth
rate assumption from 5% to 4.5% because of margin dilution, our other  key valuation
assumptions are unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium
of 6%, and a risk-free rate of 2%), and intermediate growth rate  of 14.5%.
Our bull and bear case values rise by similar magnitudes, to   NT$11,680  and NT$5,300,

respectively.

Relative valuation
Our price target of NT$8,600 implies 52x our 2027 EPS estimate, around +1 SD from
GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market

will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term, and therefore believe
that further re-rating would be justified with project wins likely to come. Despite slower
recovery of non-AI business, we continue to appreciate GUC’s growing exposure to cloud
AI semis (including AI accelerators, AI server CPU, and BMC chips).

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China  Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
PC Semis: More divergent
outlooks
Business diversification, KGD (known good die) pricing power
and supply chain management create a divergent revenue
outlook for PC semis into 4Q and likely also 1Q27. Downgrade
Elan to EW.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Elan Microelectronics Corp
(2458.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$250.00 NT$160.00
A much tougher 4Q… We mentioned in the prior PC semis note that the FOMP
(fear of missing procurement) could lead to  stable PC semis shipments in 3Q; now,

the inventory accumulation in 1Q-3Q26 could cause a deeper correction in 4Q.  PC
semis customers currently still aim for only a 5-10% Q/Q procurement decline in 4Q.
That said, the downside surprise in Parade’s 4Q outlook (link), which calls for a 20-
25% Q/Q revenue decline, could suggest a worse component correction. The still-
elevated Windows activation period of 200+ days and much smaller price hikes
from PC semis customers in 2H vs. 1H could suggest ongoing weakness into 1Q27.

But why are some companies resilient? Many investors are asking why Realtek’s
revenue could be up Q/Q in 4Q amid the PC correction (link). We cite three factors:
1) Consumer demand (other than PC/smartphones) is not necessarily all bad,
especially for IoT devices (low memory content). Espressif is guiding for  Q/Q
revenue growth in 4Q, following sequential growth in 3Q. 2) Price hikes reflect
increases at both  foundries/OSATs and in KGD. For example, TV SoC pricing is

several times higher than in past years due to  KGD price hikes (mainly DDR4). 3)
Large-scale IC design houses usually have better supply chain management and
lower exposure to PC.
No new growth driver into 2027: With the recent debate on cloud AI, we think it’s
still a matter of time before edge AI  takes off. However, we haven’t seen a clear
growth driver for PC semis. Preliminary outlooks from PC semis customers range
from flattish to down 20% Y/Y in 2027. Thus, it's  difficult to turn positive now.

Reduce earnings: We revise down Elan's PT and earnings and cut the stock to EW.
We appreciate the company's growth from smart Edge AI devices, but its exposure
to NB is still too significant to overlook. Our PT implies 16x our downward-revised
2027 EPS. Stay EW  Realtek and UW  Novatek, ASMedia, Parade, and Nuvoton.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
The FOMP (fear of missing procurement for PC semis in 1Q-3Q26) has caused a
deeper correction for some PC semis into 4Q.
It's easier for large-scale PC semis  to navigate through the downturn, while small
design houses are more concentrated on PC.
We stay conservative on PC semis, with no OW and await the cycle bottom

sometime in 2027.
November 3, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

2
Elan Micro: Earnings estimate revisions
We revise our EPS estimates by +1%/-20%/-16% for 2026/27/28: For 2026, we
incorporate actual 2Q26 results, which benefited from investment gains. For 2027-28, we
reduce our revenue and GM forecasts due to continued weak PC demand, which we
believe cannot be fully offset by the non PC business (mainly the drone market).
Exhibit 1: Elan: Estimate revisions
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates.

Exhibit 2: Elan: Quarterly financial statement
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates
Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$160: This reflects our earnings estimate revisions for
2026-28. Our price target is our base-case scenario value, which we continue to derive
from our residual income model. We reduce our intermediate growth rate estimate from
10.0% to 8.0% to reflect a weak PC business outlook. We reduce our terminal growth rate

estimate from 4.5% to 3.0% to better align it with broader economic growth. We continue
to assume a cost of equity of 9.6% and a payout ratio of 80%.
Our bull case value is cut to NT$234 from NT$366, and our bear case value is cut to NT
$84 from NT$131.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 162: 261104_daiwa_ Nien Made Enterprise

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_daiwa_ Nien Made Enterprise.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Nien Made reported its 3Q26 results on 4 November 2026 
after market hours. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our previous flash, 
2Q26 results: record-high gross margin, 12 August 2026, for more 
information).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results review: slight miss on higher opex. 
Nien Made’s 3Q26 revenue was TWD8,482m (+7.0% QoQ, +15.9% YoY), 
roughly in line with our estimate and the consensus. Its gross margin was 
60.8%, a record-high level, up from 60.0% in 2Q26 and 58.7% in 3Q25, 
beating our estimate (59.6%) and the consensus (59.8%). This was driven 
by price hikes in its custom-made segment and a lower tariff effect, despite 
being partially offset by restocking demand from ready-made products. 
However, its operating margin came in at 29.2%, up from 27.2% in 2Q26 
and the same as in 3Q25, lower than our estimate (30.3%) and the 
consensus (30.1%), due to surging fuel surcharges following oil price hikes 
in 3Q26. As such, operating profit was TWD2,473m (+14.5% QoQ, +15.9% 
YoY), missing our estimate and the consensus by 6.3% and 2.5%, 
respectively. Nien Made’s 3Q26 net profit stood at TWD1,870m (+34.5% 
QoQ, +9.6% YoY), missing our estimate and the Bloomberg consensus by 
8.9% and 4.2%, respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS came in at TWD6.38.

2027: several new initiatives in products and markets. Nien Made is on 
track to meet its 2026 target of growing its custom-made revenue in the US 
by mid-single-digit % YoY, custom-made revenue in non-US markets by 
high-single-digit % YoY and ready-made revenue by low-single-digit % YoY. 
Besides, Nien Made will continue to launch new products such as “tool-
free, drill-free” easy-install products and new blind programmes, and 
upgrade its motorised window coverings. Nien Made will continue to invest 
in its window covering brands, channels and service network (ie, European 
markets) as well as manufacturing, which should be the key factor to drive 
more order wins in the long run, in our view. We cut our 2026E EPS by 3%, 
after factoring in the 3Q26 results and keep our 2027-28E EPS almost 
unchanged.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call on Nien Made and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD520 (from TWD515), still based on a target PER 
of 20x on our 1-year forward EPS vs. its past 3-year PER trading range of 
11-21x. Key downside risks: lower-than-expected revenue from custom-
made products and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: We are more positive on the company’s leading position 
with share-gain potential. Nien Made is one of our preferred stocks in 
Taiwan’s non-tech sector.

4 November 2026

3Q26 results: record-high gross margin

➢ Despite record-high gross margin, 3Q26 net profit missed on opex 
➢ 2027: ongoing product mix optimisation trend  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising 12M TP to TWD520

Company profile 
Founded in July 2007 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in December 2015, Nien Made Enterprise 
(Nien Made) produces window coverings such as venetian blinds, shutters and shades (ie, cellular 
shades, roller blinds and roman blinds). Its major clients include dealers, designers and small specialty 
stores as well as big box retailers (ie, Home Depot and Wal-Mart).

### Page 5

5

Nien Made Enterprise (8464 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 24 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Its CEO and Chairman is the same person. Also, 3 brothers, with 1 being the CEO (Chairman)
and 2 being Vice President, are in charge of different divisions, managing and collaborating
collectively, which we view as a positive sign for solid corporate governance.
Capital management 2
Nien Made's dividend payout ratio was c.80% before 2019, but dropped to 52-71% from 2019 to
2025, which we attribute to its capacity expansion plans in Mexico and some adjustment for
COVID-19.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related-party transactions stood at c.60% of total revenue over 2022-25, with
majority from Norman International Inc. and its subsidiary. We see no evidence of suspicious
activities.
E Product design & lifecycle management 1
We note Nien Made has improved its quality, safety and efficiency throughout its product
lifecycle. The company has developed its own B2B platform for its customers for a better user
experience and efficiency. Nien Made's products have a lifespan ranging from 10 to 20 years.
Therefore, in addition to controlling production conditions through equipment such as drying kilns
and controlled-environment storage, Nien Made also focus on mitigating consumers’ health and
safety risks.
S Supply chain management 2
Nien Made's overall local-to-local raw material sourcing was at 71% in 2023, which we believe
will help reduce transportation costs in the long term. Moreover, Nien Made established supplier
management policies and relevant regulations, and its 234 suppliers signed the Supplier Code of
Conduct as of 2023.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

M
Update
Novatek  | Asia Pacific
Consumer Demand Softens as
Cost Pressure Builds
3Q growth led by smartphones: Revenue was NT$30.97bn, +8% Q/Q and +26% Y/
Y, 7% above MSe. Net income was NT$6.08bn, -4% Q/Q and +66% Y/Y, 2% above
MSe, with EPS of NT$9.99. Smartphone restocking lifted sales, but GM fell 1.53ppt
Q/Q to 40.89% on a lower SoC mix and a lag in cost pass-through. Lower opex
supported a 9.6% Q/Q rise in operating profit; net income declined in the absence of

2Q's NT$860mn one-off investment gain.
4Q revenue and margins seen declining sequentially: Management guided for
revenue of NT$28.0-29.2bn, down 6-10% Q/Q, with GM of 37-39% and OpM of 17-
19%, assuming NT$/US$32. Large DDICs should face the largest sequential revenue
drop, particularly from notebooks, followed by SoC; small/medium DDICs should
decrease slightly despite stronger Chinese OLED handset orders. Lower-cost
inventory depletion, product mix and higher memory KGD costs will likely weigh on

margins. Prices have been adjusted across SoC and DDIC products, but management
still sees higher memory prices constraining consumer demand into 2027.
New products offer growth, with cost risks unresolved: Automotive eDP TDDI is
scheduled for mass production in 4Q26, while touch-enabled notebook products are
scheduled to ramp through 2027. OLED solutions for IT applications and higher-
performance handset products broaden opportunities, although smartphone OLED

DDIC shipments are expected to decline Y/Y in 2026. Management expects new
products to support 2027 revenue growth but provided no quantified target or
assurance of flat Y/Y margins. Further foundry and OSAT price increases remain
under negotiation, leaving earnings dependent upon product mix and the pace of
cost pass-through.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Novatek (3034.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity constant at 9.8% (2.0%
risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta), payout ratio of 80%, medium-term growth rate of
6.1% and terminal growth rate assumption of 3.4%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Better-than-expected smartphone sell-through.
n Better-than-expected sell-through of foldable products.

Risks to Downside
n Intensified price competition in TDDI, driving Novatek's gross margin below its corpo-
rate average.
n Slower-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Slower sell-through of foldable products.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/11/04 投資速報

電子–半導體 
環球晶(6488.TT) 
短期獲利受火災影響，但供需吃緊及漲價趨勢不變

買進(維持評等) 
出刊緣由：法說會

個股股價報酬率 -42.4 -20.9 18.6 36.0

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
環球晶短期獲利表現低於預期，主要原因包括: (1) 義大利 Novara
廠 7 月火災影響、(2)短期產能受限，以及(3)2H26 仍適用舊LTA 價
格 。 不過最壞狀況已過 ， 隨著時序進入4Q26， 預期營收將優於3Q26，
且自 1Q27 起 ， 隨著新產能開出 ， 以及開始適用價格調漲後的新LTA，
獲利表現將明顯轉佳 。 預估2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為17.82 元
(原估 19.49 元)、33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。在投資建議上，儘管
短期營運不如預期 ， 但環球晶股價已大幅修正 ， 且2027 年半導體及
記憶體新 Fab 產能開出的趨勢明確(13 座->23 座)， 因此矽晶圓供需
轉緊且價格上漲的趨勢不變， 故維持買進建議 ， 然考量近期AI 供應
鏈出貨不順的雜音較多 ，漲價概念股評價受壓抑 ， 故將PBR 由 6X 調
整至 5X (PBR 歷史區間 2~6X，漲價循環合理評價應在中上緣 4~6X
之間)，將目標價由 1350 元下調至 1170 元。

重點摘要:

1.3Q26 財報表現不佳，主要受義大利 Novara 廠火災影響:  
3Q26 營收-2.3%QoQ(+2.6%YoY)，若排除 7 月義大利 Novara 廠火災
之影響，營收原本可以成長雙位數%QoQ。在毛利率方面，儘管3Q26
折舊費用略較前季下降 ， 但因Novara 火災導致部分高毛利率的 8 吋
SOI 基板出貨中斷 ， 導致毛利率較前季下滑0.6pt 至 20%， 不過在費
用控管下，OPM 較前季回升 0.4pt 至 9.8%。單季稅後 EPS 3.13 元，
低於原預期的 3.64 元 ， 且明顯低於前季的7.9 元 ， 差異主要係2Q26
認列較多 WAF 金融評價利益。

2.美光支付 5 億美元並非是預付款，而是認購 CB:  
今年 7 月環球晶與美光宣布簽訂 10 年期 LTA，美光將支付環球晶 5
億美元，然而，並非以預付款形式支付，而是環球晶發行上限 5 億
美元的可轉債，由美光擔任唯一應募人。研究員認為，客戶支付預
付款綁定產能的強制力相對較強 (預付款在出貨後會轉營收且不退
還)，然而，CB 存在股本稀釋的問題(推估約 3%上下)，相對較不受
歡迎。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3.2027 年起開始適用新 LTA 價格，ASP 將改善:  
在價格趨勢方面，公司對於價格調整將採取逐案談判 (Case-by-
case)，依據產品規格、供需狀況、合約到期時間與個別客戶情況進
行協議，非全產品線統一調漲特定百分比。公司認為目前市場環境
日益支持價格回歸合理水準，且已有多筆新 LTA 接近洽談完成，預
計自 2027 年起整體 ASP 將受惠於新 LTA 與產品組合優化而有所改
善。

3.4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26，但受限產能，且價格仍沿用舊合約:  
展望 4Q26，義大利 Novara 廠已正式啟動部分復工，且整體客戶需
求動能依然非常強勁 ， 甚至有客戶願意提前適用新價格以爭取產能 ，
但受限於產能不足 ， 且4Q26 絕大多數價格仍適用舊合約 ， 因此預期
4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26， 但仍無法達成雙位數%成長 。 不過自1Q27 起，
隨著新產能開出以及 ASP 提升，營運動能將轉佳。研究員預估2026
年營收-1.0%YoY，稅後EPS 17.82 元(原估 19.49 元，下修主要是因
為火災)。

4.2027 年營運將明顯好轉，大致維持獲利預估:  
展望 2027 年 ， 根據研調統計 ， 開始營運投片的新Fab 數量將自 2026
年的 13 座大幅增加至 2027 年的 23 座 ， 且成熟製程庫存健康化 ，電
源管理、車用與工業恢復，公司預期 2027 年矽晶圓供需環境將比
2026 年更吃緊。就環球晶而言，自 1Q27 起隨著新產能開出，且開
始適用調漲後的新 LTA 價格，將有助於營收及獲利轉強。此外，隨
著 AI 資料中心發展 ，SiC 市場正步入新成長週期 ，12 吋 SOI 晶圓也
受惠矽光子強勁動能推升需求，密蘇里州廠是目前美國唯一具備12
吋 SOI 製造能力的基地，產能持續爬升，客戶意向及預付款已可涵
蓋擬新增產能的 90%以上。基於以上，環球晶將步入產業上升循環，
預估 2027 年營收+33.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 29 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

Document 317: AI監管衝擊與選後美股展望-CTBC20261106

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI監管衝擊與選後美股展望-CTBC20261106.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

AI
監管衝擊與選後美股展望
2026.11.06
美股策略研究
:

厲柏宏

### Page 2

結論與展望
• AI監管風險：民主黨取得國會兩院控制權，AI監管疑慮升溫。2027年資料中心建設進度相對
穩定，但2028年約30GW規劃容量面臨潛在審批風險，恐影響部分專案投產時程。
• 產業影響預期：德州共和黨仍掌握州參議院多數，長期全面禁建面臨立法及司法制衡。過去
約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延期僅約1.5GW，顯示政策曝險不等於實際損失。
• 政策影響：民主黨可能透過國會制衡財政擴張，中長期若赤字及發債壓力下降，將有利美債
表現。美元預期短空長多，而美伊衝突降溫預期升高，可能使短期油價持續承壓。
• 醫療與零售：民主黨可能爭取恢復部分Medicaid、SNAP及ACA補貼，有助維持中低收入家
庭購買力，支撐消費與醫療需求；惟川普仍掌握關稅行政權，零售業成本改善空間相對有限。
• 美股後市展望：歷史經驗顯示，期中選舉後隨政治不確定性下降，美股通常呈現回升。過去9
次期中選舉後12個月，科技股有8次跑贏大盤，獲利品質、成長及低波動策略亦具相對優勢，
後續仍須關注AI監管落地及企業獲利表現。

1資料來源：中信投顧整理

### Page 3

AI監管提案增加，2028面臨較高審批風險
• 民主黨在期中選舉取得參眾兩院 ，並提出超級 AI禁令及資料中心暫停建設法案，分別涉及限
制AI模型開發，及暫停新建資料中心直至相關機制建立 ，引發對監管趨嚴與投資放緩的擔憂 。
• SemiAnalysis估計，美國2027預計交付的資料中心約有75%已動工，短期進度穩定；惟2028，
規劃多數尚未施工，若暫停建設政策擴大，約30GW新增容量可能面臨審批延後風險。
2資料來源：dcmap.us、中信投顧整理
AI監管風險
美國各州資料中心容量分布與建設進度 (GW)

### Page 4

州級監管受立法制衡，政策曝險不等於實際損失
• 州長行政權仍受州議會及司法制衡 ，以德州為例，共和黨仍掌握州參議院過半席次，全面禁
建制度化仍需跨黨派支持；惟州政府可延續部分許可限制，短期專案進度仍可能受影響。
• 過去一年約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延誤僅約 1.5GW；預期監管趨嚴將提高開
發成本並延後部分2028專案，短期影響AI硬體訂單與交付，但長期算力需求未必減少。
3資料來源：MultiState Elctions、SemiAnalysis、中信投顧整理
產業影響預期
11/05德州期中選舉統計 地方禁令實際影響有限

### Page 5

國會制衡與美伊局勢緩和，牽動美債及美元走勢
• 民主黨掌控國會後，較可能透過立法及預算權制衡共和黨政策 ，降低進一步財政擴張空間。
短期政策不確定性可能加大債市波動 ，但中長期若赤字與發債壓力下降，將有利美債表現。
而AI投資貢獻美國經濟成長比例加大，故美元表現將與AI股趨勢相同，預期美元短空長多。
• 伊朗戰爭推升汽油價格成為共和黨選舉壓力來源 ，隨民主黨掌控國會提高延續軍事行動的政
治成本，加上美伊持續透過中東國家間接接觸，市場對衝突降溫預期升高，油價短期回落。
4資料來源：中信投顧整理
政策影響
美國總統與國會權力

### Page 6

國會控制權轉向，醫療與消費政策迎來調整空間
• 民主黨可能透過債務上限協商，爭取延後或縮減 Medicaid、SNAP減支，並恢復ACA增額保
費補貼，有助維持中低收入家庭購買力與醫療保險覆蓋率，對民生消費、醫療服務偏正面。
• 關稅也是本次輪替重點，但川普仍握有貿易政策行政權與301條款等工具，整體有效關稅稅率
未必明顯下降；因此零售業獲利改善仍取決於企業定價能力及供應鏈調整成效。
5資料來源：Commonwealth Fund、Uni. of Penn、中信投顧整理
醫療與零售
現行法規預期刪減福利 美國現行關稅預估

### Page 7

期中選舉後政治不確定性下降，歷史上美股通常回升
• 觀察過去數次美國期中選舉，美股通常在選前因政治不確定性升高而面臨較大波動 ，但選後
隨著政策方向逐漸明朗，期中選舉後標普多數呈現上漲趨勢。
• 產業方面，科技股在過去9次期中選舉後的12個月，科技股有8次跑贏標普。平均報酬最高的
選股策略則為獲利品質、成長與低波動類股。
6資料來源：JPM、Barclays、中信投顧整理
歷史統計
標普在期中選舉年表現 各投資策略及產業表現

Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 355: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

STRONG RESILIENCE TO EXTERNAL SHOCKS
, 
SOLID 
MACROECONOMIC FUNDAMENTALS 
◼ 
 Over the past 
 two years
 , India has demonstrated remarkable resilience in the face of external shocks, while its 
macroeconomic fundamentals have remained solid.  
◼ 
 In FY2025/2026  
(ended in March 2026)
 , India was hit by a sharp rise in US tariffs. However, thanks to supportive fiscal 
and monetary policies, 
 real GDP  
grew by 7.8% in FY2026, placing India among the fastest
 -
 growing Asian economies.  
◼ 
 For the current fiscal year, the Indian economy is facing rising energy prices 
 (
due to 
 the conflict in the Middle East and 
increased food prices 
 because 
 of 
an 
unfavourable monsoon
 ) 
as well as 
 monetary tightening. Nevertheless, despite these 
new challenges, real GDP growth is expected to exceed 7%.  
◼ 
 India is currently in a stronger position than it was in 2022 to cope with the new energy shock. The fiscal leeway to support  
the economy has increased with the decline in the fiscal deficit, and inflation is more contained.  
◼ 
 Although 
 rising  
energy prices have put pressure on external accounts, FX reserves 
 remain  
sufficient to cover the country’s 
short
 -
 term external financing needs. 
 Yet, 
downward pressures on the rupee have been 
 significant 
 and 
could persist in the 
short term
 . 
In 
H1 
2026, India’s external accounts withstood the rise in energy prices fairly well
 , notably  
thanks to the sharp increase 
in workers’ remittances. 
 T
he 
widening of the trade deficit was primarily due to the substantial increase in machinery and 
equipment imports 
 while t
 he oil trade deficit increased 
 only 
moderately, as the rise in energy prices was offset by a decline 
in import volumes. 
 However, 
in H2 2026, the oil trade deficit is expected to widen significantly.  
Additionally, large portfolio outflows have exacerbated downward pressure on the rupee, necessitating interventions by 
the central bank in foreign exchange markets and the adoption of regulatory measures to support the currency. Although 
these measures hav
 e led to a rebound in the rupee (at least temporarily), inflationary pressures, volatile portfolio 
investments, and low net foreign direct investments are 
 expected to continue 
 to put pressure on the currency.  
◼ 
 D
ownward pressures on the rupee 
 and 
 rising bond yields will reduce the government's 
 already limited 
 fiscal capacity to 
increase public investment and 
 support economic development. However, this will not undermine its ability to refinance 
its debt, given its safe structure.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector is sufficiently robust to cope with the expected rise in credit risks.

STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH DESPITE 
STRUCTURAL CONSTRAINTS 
◼ 
 In October 2026, the IMF revised upward 
 India’s  
medium
 -
 term growth 
 forecast 
 from 6.5% to 6.8%  
per year
 . Nonetheless, 
to achieve such strong growth performance, 
 India will have to adapt to the challenges posed by artificial intelligence  
(AI)
, 
while its infrastructure and education system are not prepared for it.  
◼ 
 Moreover, India’s development remains highly uneven. 
 The middle class is still modest
 . This is explained by a large 
informal economy, heavy concentration of employment in low value
 -
 added sectors such as agriculture, low participation 
rates among women and youth, and limited industrial development. All this, in turn, reflects India’s weak i
 ntegration into 
global value chains.  
◼ 
 India faces two key structural challenges: i
 ) adapting the services sector to AI and ii) accelerating industrial development. 
Even with progress in sectors such as smartphones and pharmaceuticals, India’s industrial base remains too small to 
absorb its vast labour force. 
 Labo
u
r market 
 rigidities  
and the mismatch between training and business needs are holding 
back both the creation of quality jobs and industrial development.  
◼ 
 Low net foreign direct investment is a 
 major  
 obstacle. While 
 India attracts 
 new 
 FDI, retention is weak
 : 
 despite 
improvements in logistics, governance issues and regulatory barriers have 
 led 
 disinvestments to triple over the past five 
years.  
◼ 
 India 
 may fail in completing its 
 transition to a higher
 -
 value, more industrialised and inclusive economy if reforms do not 
accelerate 
 significantly
 . Structural change
 s 
could lag behind pressures from its rapidly growing population and 
 fast 
global 
technological advances
 .

INDIA
’S COMPLEX POSITION 
IN INTERNATIONAL TRADE 
Low 
attractiveness  
due to unfavourable business environment and limited integration in global value chains  
◼ 
 Despite very strong growth, India's attractiveness is low relative to the size of its economy. The stock of foreign direct 
investment stood at only 15% of GDP in Q2 2026 compared to 52% of GDP in Vietnam and around 67% of GDP in Thailand 
and Malaysia.  
◼ 
 This lack of appeal can be attributed to 
 India’s still unfavo
 u
rable 
 business environment and 
 its 
 limited integration into 
global value chains. India’s global market share remain
 s 
 low (1.9% of total 
 world 
 exports  
 of goods
 )
, 
 especially in 
comparison with that of China (16.1% of total exports).  
◼ 
 India has not benefited from the AI boom
 , 
since its exports of AI
 -
 related goods remain modest (0.5% of its total exports)
 . 
However, 
 it has benefited from the “China +1” strategy adopted by 
 international 
 companies seeking to diversify their 
production sources. Smartphone exports by India have increased 6.3
 -
 fold over the past five years and accounted for almost 
6% of global exports in 2025 compared to less than 1% five year
 s 
earlier.  
To 
increase its a
 ttractiveness and develop its industrial sector, India has 
 multiplied F
 ree 
T
rade 
 A
greements  
◼ 
 Over the past 18 months, 
 India  
has signed a growing number of free trade agreements 
 – 
with the European Free Trade 
Association in October 2025, the United Kingdom in July 2025, Oman in December 2025, and the European Union in January 
2026
. This 
 should help to develop the industrial base and demonstrates the Modi government's commitment to 
accelerating India's integration into global trade.  
The sectors most likely to benefit from the recent trade agreement with the EU are electronics and semiconductors, 
pharmaceuticals and chemical
 s
, textile and renewable energy technologies. But expanding trade agreements without deep 
domestic improvements in the business environment, governance, education, and infrastructure will be ineffective. India 
will continue to struggle with investment reten
 tion. 
◼ 
 The US
 -
 India bilateral trade agreement has not yet been concluded. The two sides reached a framework for an interim 
Agreement in February 2026, under which the US would apply an 18% reciprocal tariff on Indian goods, with further 
reductions subject to the 
 successful conclusion of the agreement. As of early October, negotiations 
 are 
stalled. Key sticking 
points include market access, particularly 
 in 
India’s sensitive agricultural sector.

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE

Non transfer risk   
India’s external accounts are solid with a low external debt, moderate current account deficit and large FX 
reserves. 
 India’s net debtor position reached only 5.9% of GDP in Q2 2026. In the short term, external accounts 
are expected to deteriorate 
 given 
the rise in energy prices while downward pressures on the rupee should remain 
high.  
◼ 
 External accounts have remained solid 
 despite recent adverse shocks
 . 
 In 2025, external accounts remained solid 
despite the significant increase in US tariffs. In 
 H1 
 2026, they held up well against rising energy prices. Although the 
current account deficit is expected to widen for the current fiscal year as a whole, it should remain contained, and foreign 
exchange reserves should be more than sufficient to cover the co
 untry’s short
 -
 term financing needs. The 
 major  
causes 
for concern are the 
 structurally 
 low level of foreign direct investment (although it is rising) and the increased reliance on 
portfolio investment, which exposes the currency to greater volatility.  
◼ 
 Structural vulnerability to energy price shock
 . 
India is structurally vulnerable to an energy price shock as its 
 oil and gas  
trade deficit is relatively high (2.8% of GDP in 2025). However, in H1 2026, the current account withstood fairly well the 
rise in energy prices especially thanks to the sharp increase in workers’ remittances  
(+0.7 pp to 3.7% of GDP)
 . The trade 
deficit widened significantly but principally because of the strong increase in machinery and equipment imports rather

 energy

decline in imported volumes.  
However, the trade deficit is expected to widen further during the rest of the year with the significant rise in oil prices, 
and 
 the current account deficit 
 is projected

 1.

 This is

 .

 .

 External debt is low (20.8% of GDP and 87.1% of total exports in Q2 2026).

 Currency risk
 : 
 India is vulnerable to capital outflows and is very exposed to change in US monetary policy. 
 Its

Sovereign risk

 and fiscal policy room is narrow, 
 as India has one of the lowest tax bases

risk is limited given 
 its 
sound 
 profile 
 (
debt 
is 
denominated in domestic currency and held by domestic investors

◼ 
 In FY2026 (ended in March 2026), public finances continued to improve. 
 The central government’s fiscal deficit 
narrowed to 4.4% of GDP from 5% of GDP the previous year. 
 However, t
 his reduction stemmed primarily from a significant 
rise in dividends from the central bank. 
 G
overnment revenues remained low at  
9.6% of GDP, with tax revenues stagnant 
at 
7.6% of GDP. Besides, debt
 -
 servicing costs climbed to a peak of  
3.6% of GDP, equivalent to  
37.6% of total government 
revenue
s

The fiscal position is stronger than during the 2022 energy crisis, with the general government deficit estimated 
at 
7.8% of GDP  
in FY2026, compared to  
9.5% 
in FY2021/2022.  
◼ 
 For FY2027, the initial budget targets a slight reduction in the central 
 government 
 deficit, to 4.3% of GDP. This goal may 
however 
 prove challenging due to rising subsidies amid the energy shock.  
To cushion households from the shock, the government initially 
 kept 
petrol prices 
 unchanged
 . Refining firms absorbed the 
financial burden, partially offset by lower excise duties on petroleum products. Only after regional elections in mid
 -
 May 
did the government allow a modest increase in pump prices to reflect higher international 
 prices
 . 
The government has 
also increased subsidies  
to offset the impact of rising fertiliser prices on farmers.  
Revenue losses  
—
driven by  
lower excise 
duties, reduced dividends from state
 -
 owned enterprises, and the economic slowdown
 — 
could reach  
0.5% of GDP
 , while 
the cost of 
 fertiliser subsidies 
 could 
 rise from  
0.4% to 0.8% of GDP.  
To reduce the risk of fiscal slippage, the government 
has: i) raised taxes on fuel exports, ii) capped household diesel purchases; and iii) planned cuts to certain expenditures.  
◼ 
 While the primary deficit reduction  
 target may prove difficult to achieve, 
 the surge in government bond yields  
 is 
another source of severe concern
 . 
Over the first ten months of 2026
 , 
10

 and 
further increase  
is 
expected due to 
 the central bank’s 
 monetary tightening. A  
100
-
 bp 
rise in interest rates  
could push debt  
servicing costs above  
40% of 
fiscal 
 revenue by 2029
 . This is expected to 
 increase the debt
 -
 to
-
 GDP ratio 
 gradually
 .

◼ 
 Despite the high public debt level (84.6% of GDP in FY2026), refinancing risks remain moderate. 
 The debt structure is 
relatively safe, and the government benefits from access to 
 large 
 domestic savings. In Q2 2026, 87.8% of central 
government debt was domestic
 , and 
 70.5% was market debt
 . 
It is 
 almost entirely held by domestic financial institutions 
and denominated in local currency. 
 R
efinancing risks are limited as most debt is long term (the average maturity of central

 -

 Treasury

 Finally,

 in foreign currency 
 accounted 
for only 3.0% of total general government debt in FY2025/2026 (
 i.e.

Systemic credit risk  
The banking sector has strengthened over the past five years
 , 
as illustrated by the improvement in 
 asset  
quality 
and capital buffers. The main sources of credit risks are rising household debt, the rise in energy prices, the 
unfavourable monsoon and monetary tightening.  
◼ 
 The quality of assets has improved significantly. 
 In Q1 2026, the banking sector’s non
 -
 performing loan (NPL) ratio fell 
to a multi
 -
 decade low of 1.8%. For public banks  
(
54.4% of total loans
 )
, the NPL ratio declined to 2.1%.  
◼ 
 Capital buffers are strong. 
 While 
loan loss provisions remained modest at 75.6% of NPLs, capital buffers stayed strong. 
The Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR) rose to 17.7% in Q1 2026, up from 17.4% a year earlier. From April 2027, Indian banks 
will adopt a new Expected Credit Loss (ECL) framewo
 rk, requiring earlier provisioning for potential loan losses. In the 
short term, higher provisioning requirements could push up lending rates and tighten credit conditions 
 while 
 triggering 
capital raising or targeted government recapitalisation. 
 I
n the medium term, the reform could strengthen banking  
sector 
resilience through earlier recognition of credit risks and more robust loss buffers.  
◼ 
 Banks’ 
 liquidity 
 is 
 comfortable
 . 
It deteriorated slightly in H1 2026 because credit growth outpaced deposit growth
 ; t
he 
liquidity coverage ratio decli
 ned to 
a still solid 
 124% 
and 
the loan
 -
 to
-
 deposit ratio increased to 83% in Q2 2026 from 79.1% 
a year earlier. RBI interventions to support the rupee 
 also 
 drained liquidity
 . Moreover
 , the deposit mix shifted from low
 -
cost current and savings accounts (CASA) to higher
 -
 cost term deposits, pressuring net interest margins. However, since 
June 2026, liquidity has improved significantly due to RBI measures to attract foreign
 -
 currency non
 -
 re
sident (FCNR) 
deposits.  
◼ 
 Profitability remains satisfactory. 
 It 
 has declined since the start of the year, primarily due to narrowing net interest 
margins, but remains satisfactory. In Q2 2026, 
 the 
 return on assets (ROA) and return on equity (ROE) stood at 1.6% and 
13.0%, respectively (IMF data).  
◼ 
 P
rivate
 -
 sector  
 debt 
 is moderate
 , 
 but its increase 
 needs to be monitored.  
 It stood 
 at 105.1% of GDP in Q1 2026 (BIS  
data
), compared to 100.1% five years earlier
 , which is 
 lower than in other emerging Asian economies.  
Non
-
 financial corporat
 e 
debt was 56% of GDP in Q1 2026. Listed firms’ financial positions continued to strengthen amid 
surging profits, improving their ability to service debt. In Q2 2026, the 
 average 
 interest coverage ratio (ICR) rose to 7.4, up 
from 6.1 a year earlier. However, the share of companies with an ICR below 1 (insufficient earnings to cover interest costs) 
increased to 18.5%. Stress remain
 s 
 concentrated in micro
 -
 enterprises, agriculture, and energy
 -
 intensive sectors (
 e.g.
, 
power, steel, cement, aviation), which are 
 the 
most exposed to oil
 -
 price shocks.  
Household leverage requires monitoring. Although still moderate, household debt increased to 49.1% of GDP in Q1 2026, 
up from 42.7% five years earlier, driven largely by non
 -
 housing retail loans (58.4% of total borrowing).  
◼ 
 Monetary 
 policy 
 tightening. 
 Due to rising  
inflation (+5.5% year
 -
 on
-
 year in September) and downward pressure on the 
rupee, the RBI raised its policy rate by 25 basis points to 5.5% in October 2026 under a "calibrated tightening" stance.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector should remain resilient  
 despite rising risks in the short term. 
 Due to rising energy prices, a poor 
monsoon, and monetary tightening, asset quality is expected to deteriorate, particularly for low
 -
 income borrowers, micro, 
and small enterprises. 
 E
arly warning signs are emerging
 , with some 
 borrowers starting to miss payments. The stress is 
concentrated in unsecured retail loans  
(
which account for over half of fresh retail slippages
 )
. 
This could lead to new 
 NPLs 
over the coming quarters, especially as higher rates and inflation squeeze repayment capacity. Additionally, in the near 
term, capital inflows may remain weak, 
 and 
 liquidity and funding pressures could intensify.

Document 382: First Impressions_ NZ labour market surveys, September quarter 2026 (2026-11-10)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ labour market surveys, September quarter 2026 (2026-11-10).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ labour market surveys, September quarter
2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:01 November 04 2026 Share
The unemployment rate rose to 5.7% in the September quarter, against expectations for a
small decline. Spare capacity in the labour market is keeping wage pressures subdued.
Unemployment rate: 5.7% (prev: 5.6%, Westpac: 5.5%, RBNZ: 5.5%, mkt: 5.5%)
Employment change: -0.1% (prev: +0.3%, Westpac: +0.5%, RBNZ: +0.3%, mkt: +0.4%)

### Page 2

Participation rate: 70.4% (prev: 70.6%, Westpac: 70.8%, RBNZ: 70.6%, mkt: 70.7%) 
Labour costs (private sector): +0.5% (prev: +0.7%, Westpac: +0.5%, RBNZ: +0.4%, mkt: +0.5%)

The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in New Zealand’s labour market. The
unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an almost five-year run of increases.
Employment was softer than expected, and wage growth remains subdued despite increasing cost-of-
living pressures. 
The Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS) showed a surprise 0.1% fall in employment in the
September quarter, after a downwardly revised 0.3% increase in the June quarter. That went against
the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI) measure of filled jobs, which had suggested some upward
momentum emerging in the jobs market (and notably, the HLFS measure had outpaced the MEI for the
previous nine quarters). It was also softer than suggested by the business-focused Quarterly
Employment Survey (QES), which recorded a 0.2% rise in full-time equivalent employees, a 0.1% rise
in filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. 
The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in labour force participation rate, from 70.6%
to 70.4%. These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were indicative of sampling error in the survey. 
This is why we generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, as it’s less vulnerable to
sampling error. And in that respect, the message on the jobs market remains weak: to the extent that
employment may be rising, it’s still not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population (which is
itself a fairly low bar, as net migration remains at a relatively low ebb). 
The HLFS also recorded a sharp 1.6% fall in hours worked (although usual hours worked were down
just 0.4%). This seems to have been influenced by Matariki – a floating holiday that was only
introduced a few years ago, so will not be picked up by the seasonal adjustment. This may have also
been responsible for a marked shift from full-time to part-time employment, as some people may have
been pushed into the part-time category simply by virtue of only working four days during the survey
week. 
The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter, in line with our expectations
overall but a bit weaker in the details – private sector wages were up by 0.5%, while there was a
surprisingly large 0.7% increase in public sector wages. There was a notable jump in the number of
employers citing cost-of-living adjustments as a reason for increasing pay rates, although the size of
those increases varied a great deal, with many substantially less than the headline inflation rate. 
Overall, we think the details are soft from the RBNZ’s perspective – an important consideration ahead
of the 9 December Monetary Policy Statement, as there are few other major data releases between
now and then. The unemployment rate is a more reliable signal than the employment measures, and
the ongoing rise in unemployment points to more slack in the labour market than the RBNZ was hoping
to see in an economy that has been growing for over a year now. The doves on the policy committee
will be encouraged in their view that there is limited scope for headline inflation shocks to pass through
into second-round effects. 
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist
P: +64 21 749 506
